## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can get a broken test in one of your repos fixed while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 예를 들어, 깨진 저장소의 테스트를 고쳐 달라고 하시면 제가 작업을 맡아 진행하고 결과를 알려 드릴게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 좋아요, 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요. 편하게 이어서 말씀하세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性を重視した届け方」、UDPは「速さを重視した届け方」です。

TCPは相手に届いたか確認し、届かなければ再送し、順番も保証します。UDPは確認も再送もせず、送りっぱなしにするので遅延が少ないです。Webや メールはTCP、動画の配信やオンラインゲームのような速さが大事な用途はUDPが向いています。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner tu rama local al día con la base o limpiar tus propios commits antes de compartirlos, porque deja un historial lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o pública, porque reescribir commits que otros ya tienen rompe su trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有连接、按字节流传输的；UDP 则不保证送达、不建连接，只管尽快把数据包发出去。
```

### direct-en

--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径就行，把它放在 `bun test` 后面：

```sh
bun test tests/foo.test.ts
```

Bun 会把参数当成文件路径过滤，所以写文件名的一部分也能匹配，比如 `bun test foo`。

如果想只跑某个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```sh
bun test tests/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例的名字"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change to the data is written down before it is applied to the database itself. If the system crashes midway, the database can replay that log on restart to recover the changes that hadn't yet reached the main data files.
```

### past-rust-haswell-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 我上个月在 haswell 上那个 Rust 构建的，再接着把增量构建也弄快

  [sent to task rust_haswell] 用户说："我上个月在 haswell 上那个 Rust 构建的，再接着把增量构建也弄快"

已完成的部分：冷构建从 14 分钟降到 6 分钟（sccache + mold 链接）。增量构建还没动。

本次要做：
- 在 haswell 上把 Rust 增量构建也提速（改一个文件后再次构建的时间）。
- 先量出当前增量构建耗时，再动手改；改完再量一次，前后数字都报告。
- 可以考虑的方向：增量编译相关的 cargo 设置、sccache 的缓存命中、链接器、target 目录复用等，以实测为准。

完成标准：报告增量构建改前改后的耗时（有命令输出作证据），并确认冷构建没有变慢。

    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="open">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">tap a PR to tick it off when it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Open PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row { cursor: pointer; align-items: center; }
  .k-row.is-done .k-row-main { text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--d-muted); }
  .k-tick { width: 22px; height: 22px; border-radius: 6px; border: 1.5px solid var(--d-line); display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; margin-right: 10px; flex: none; font-size: 14px; color: var(--d-good); }
  .k-row.is-done .k-tick { border-color: var(--d-good); background: var(--d-sunken); }
</style>
<script>
  var DEFAULT = ducter.get("default") || [];
  var items = DEFAULT;

  function countOpen() { return items.filter(function (it) { return !it.done; }).length; }

  function render() {
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    items.forEach(function (it, i) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row' + (it.done ? ' is-done' : '');
       … xt.appendChild(main);
      text.appendChild(sub);
      row.appendChild(tick);
      row.appendChild(text);
      row.addEventListener('click', function () { toggle(i); });
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    var open = countOpen();
    document.getElementById('open').textContent = String(open);
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = open === 0
      ? 'all landed'
      : 'tap a PR to tick it off when it lands';
    if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined' && ducter.summary) {
      ducter.summary(open + ' still waiting');
    }
  }

  function save() {
    if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined' && ducter.set) { ducter.set('prs', items); }
  }

  function toggle(i) {
    items[i].done = !items[i].done;
    save();
    render();
  }

  if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined' && ducter.get) {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('prs')).then(function (v) {
      if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length) { items = v; }
      render();
    });
    if (ducter.onChange) {
      ducter.onChange(function () {
        Promise.resolve(ducter.get('prs')).then(function (v) {
          if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length) { items = v; render(); }
        });
      });
    }
  } else {
    render();
  }
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"412","title":"Compaction cut","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#412","note":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday","done":false},{"id":"10287","title":"Retry on 502","ref":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","note":"CI is failing","done":false},{"id":"1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","ref":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","note":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"418","title":"Apps bar","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#418","note":"Draft, not yet asked for review","done":false}],"default":[{"id":"412","title":"Compaction cut","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#412","note":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Monday","done":false},{"id":"10287","title":"Retry on 502","ref":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","note":"CI is failing","done":false},{"id":"1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","ref":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","note":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","done":false},{"id":"418","title":"Apps bar","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#418","note":"Draft, not yet asked for review","done":false}]}

    '<div class="k-row-value">' + money(x.amt) + '</div></div>'
  ).join("");

}
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => {

});
$("save").onclick = () => {

};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个奶油色的 3D 风格笔记本电脑插画：屏幕空白，边框是深藏青色，键盘有按键细节，背景透明。

适合放在空状态里。空白屏幕本身就表示"还没有内容"，配色柔和，看起来亲切，不会让人觉得出了错。不过屏幕完全空白时，有人可能会以为页面没加载好，可以在屏幕上放个小图标或一行字，再配一句引导文案。另外它只有 320×246，放大用可能会糊，最好准备一张 2 倍的图。深色模式下奶油色会比较亮，最好实际看一下效果。
